Comprehensive experimental results on multiple cross-domain benchmarks demonstrate that AoD-IP maintains strong authorized-domain performance and reliable unauthorized detection, while supporting user-controlled authorization for adaptive deployment in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [155] [EvoTool: Self-Evolving Tool-Use Policy Optimization in LLM Agents via Blame-Aware Mutation and Diversity-Aware Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04900)
*Shuo Yang,Soyeon Caren Han,Xueqi Ma,Yan Li,Mohammad Reza Ghasemi Madani,Eduard Hovy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoTool：基于进化范式的自进化框架，通过模块化分解和针对性突变优化LLM智能体的工具使用策略，解决信用分配和错误传播问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体工具使用策略优化方法存在局限性：整体式方法容易导致行为纠缠，单方面方法忽略跨模块错误传播。需要解决延迟监督和长轨迹信用分配的挑战

Method: 将工具使用策略分解为四个模块（规划器、选择器、调用器、合成器），通过轨迹基础的责任归属、反馈引导的针对性突变、多样性感知的种群选择三个机制进行迭代优化

Result: 在四个基准测试中，EvoTool在GPT-4.1和Qwen3-8B上均优于强基线5个点以上，同时实现更高的效率和可迁移性

Conclusion: EvoTool通过模块化分解和进化优化有效解决了LLM智能体工具使用策略的优化问题，为复杂任务中的工具使用提供了有效的自进化框架

Abstract: LLM-based agents depend on effective tool-use policies to solve complex tasks, yet optimizing these policies remains challenging due to delayed supervision and the difficulty of credit assignment in long-horizon trajectories. Existing optimization approaches tend to be either monolithic, which are prone to entangling behaviors, or single-aspect, which ignore cross-module error propagation. To address these limitations, we propose EvoTool, a self-evolving framework that optimizes a modular tool-use policy via a gradient-free evolutionary paradigm. EvoTool decomposes agent's tool-use policy into four modules, including Planner, Selector, Caller, and Synthesizer, and iteratively improves them in a self-improving loop through three novel mechanisms. Trajectory-Grounded Blame Attribution uses diagnostic traces to localize failures to a specific module. Feedback-Guided Targeted Mutation then edits only that module via natural-language critique. Diversity-Aware Population Selection preserves complementary candidates to ensure solution diversity. Across four benchmarks, EvoTool outperforms strong baselines by over 5 points on both GPT-4.1 and Qwen3-8B, while achieving superior efficiency and transferability. The code will be released once paper is accepted.

</details>


### [156] [Alignment Backfire: Language-Dependent Reversal of Safety Interventions Across 16 Languages in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04904)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型对齐干预会产生类似犯罪者治疗中的"洞察与行动分离"现象：表面安全掩盖集体病理和内部解离，且效果因语言文化而异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于观察到大语言模型对齐干预可能产生与犯罪者治疗中类似的"洞察与行动分离"现象，即模型表面显示安全行为但实际产生集体病理，且这种效应可能因语言文化背景不同而存在差异

Method: 采用四个预注册研究，共1584个多智能体模拟，涵盖16种语言和三个模型家族。研究1测试英语和日语中的对齐效果；研究2扩展到16种语言，分析文化语言因素；研究3测试个体化作为对策；研究4在Llama 3.3 70B、GPT-4o-mini和Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B上验证模式

Result: 研究发现：1) 对齐干预在英语中减少集体病理但在日语中加剧（对齐反噬）；2) 对齐诱导的解离在15/16语言中普遍存在；3) 集体病理沿文化语言线分叉，与权力距离指数相关；4) 个体化智能体成为病理和解离的主要来源；5) 英语安全具有模型通用性，日语反噬具有模型特异性

Conclusion: 对齐应被重新定义为受风险稳态和医源性效应影响的行为干预。语言空间（训练数据继承的语言、语用和文化属性）结构性地决定对齐结果。英语验证的安全无法转移到其他语言，提示级干预无法覆盖语言空间级约束

Abstract: In perpetrator treatment, a recurring observation is the dissociation between insight and action: offenders articulate remorse yet behavioral change does not follow. We report four preregistered studies (1,584 multi-agent simulations across 16 languages and three model families) demonstrating that alignment interventions in large language models produce a structurally analogous phenomenon: surface safety that masks or generates collective pathology and internal dissociation. In Study 1 (N = 150), increasing alignment-instructed agents reduced collective pathology in English (g = -1.844, p < .0001) but amplified it in Japanese (g = +0.771, p = .038)--a directional reversal we term "alignment backfire." Study 2 (N = 1,174) extended to 16 languages: alignment-induced dissociation was near-universal (15/16 languages; beta = 0.0667, p < .0001), while collective pathology bifurcated along cultural-linguistic lines (interaction beta = 0.0684, p = .0003), correlating with Power Distance Index (r = 0.474, p = .064). Study 3 (N = 180) tested individuation as countermeasure; individuated agents became the primary source of both pathology and dissociation (DI = +1.120) with conformity above 84%--demonstrating iatrogenesis. Study 4 (N = 80) validated patterns across Llama 3.3 70B, GPT-4o-mini, and Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B, confirming English safety is model-general while Japanese backfire is model-specific. These findings reframe alignment as a behavioral intervention subject to risk homeostasis and iatrogenesis. Language space--the linguistic, pragmatic, and cultural properties inherited from training data--structurally determines alignment outcomes. Safety validated in English does not transfer to other languages, and prompt-level interventions cannot override language-space-level constraints.

</details>


### [157] [Knowledge-informed Bidding with Dual-process Control for Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04920)
*Huixiang Luo,Longyu Gao,Yaqi Liu,Qianqian Chen,Pingchun Huang,Tianning Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KBD：基于知识引导和双过程控制的竞价优化方法，将人类专家经验作为归纳偏置，结合决策变换器进行全局优化，通过PID快速规则系统与DT系统2实现双过程控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线广告竞价优化依赖黑盒机器学习模型，无法复现人类专家的自适应、经验驱动和全局一致的决策能力。具体问题包括：1) 数据稀疏情况下泛化能力差（缺乏结构化知识）；2) 短视的序列决策（忽略长期依赖）；3) 分布外场景适应能力不足（而人类专家能成功应对）。

Method: 提出KBD方法：1) 通过知情机器学习范式将人类专家经验嵌入为归纳偏置；2) 使用决策变换器全局优化多步竞价序列；3) 实现双过程控制，结合快速规则基础的PID系统（系统1）和DT系统（系统2）。

Result: 大量实验表明KBD优于现有方法，强调了将竞价优化建立在人类专业知识和双过程控制基础上的优势。

Conclusion: KBD通过整合人类专家知识和双过程控制机制，有效解决了传统黑盒模型在数据稀疏、长期依赖和分布外场景下的局限性，为在线广告竞价优化提供了更鲁棒和自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Bid optimization in online advertising relies on black-box machine-learning models that learn bidding decisions from historical data. However, these approaches fail to replicate human experts' adaptive, experience-driven, and globally coherent decisions. Specifically, they generalize poorly in data-sparse cases because of missing structured knowledge, make short-sighted sequential decisions that ignore long-term interdependencies, and struggle to adapt in out-of-distribution scenarios where human experts succeed. To address this, we propose KBD (Knowledge-informed Bidding with Dual-process control), a novel method for bid optimization. KBD embeds human expertise as inductive biases through the informed machine-learning paradigm, uses Decision Transformer (DT) to globally optimize multi-step bidding sequences, and implements dual-process control by combining a fast rule-based PID (System 1) with DT (System 2). Extensive experiments highlight KBD's advantage over existing methods and underscore the benefit of grounding bid optimization in human expertise and dual-process control.

</details>


### [158] [Rethinking Representativeness and Diversity in Dynamic Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04981)
*Yuzhe Zhou,Zhenglin Hua,Haiyun Guo,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态数据选择新框架，重新定义样本评估中的代表性和多样性概念，通过稀疏自编码器特征空间评分和使用频率惩罚机制，实现2倍以上训练加速同时保持或超越全数据精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态数据选择方法通常基于局部几何中心性定义代表性，基于子集内分散度定义多样性。本文认为这些定义不够准确，需要重新思考样本评估的两个核心概念：代表性应关注数据集级别的常见或高频特征因子覆盖，多样性应在过程层面要求选择轨迹逐步包含互补的稀有因子。

Method: 提出三组件动态选择框架：1）在插件特征空间中评分代表性，使用在目标数据集上训练的稀疏自编码器，通过稀疏单元激活总结个体样本和数据集级因子统计；2）通过稀有因子采样结合使用频率惩罚实现过程级多样性，该惩罚机制促进样本轮换、理论上阻止垄断并减少梯度偏差；3）将二维评分与平滑调度器耦合，实现从核心模式巩固到稀有因子探索的过渡，无需额外梯度、影响估计或训练模型的二阶计算。

Result: 在五个视觉和文本任务基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在模型间改进了精度-效率权衡。方法匹配或超越了全数据精度，同时实现了超过2倍的训练加速。

Conclusion: 通过重新定义代表性和多样性概念，提出了一种有效的动态数据选择框架，能够在保持模型性能的同时显著加速训练过程。该方法无需复杂的计算开销，在多个任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic data selection accelerates training by sampling a changing subset of the dataset while preserving accuracy. We rethink two core notions underlying sample evaluation: representativeness and diversity. Instead of local geometric centrality, we define representativeness as coverage of dataset-level common or high-frequency feature factors. Instead of within-subset dispersion, we define diversity at the process level, requiring the selection trajectory to gradually include complementary rare factors over training. Based on this view, we propose a dynamic selection framework with three components. First, we score representativeness in a plug-in feature space to prioritize samples covering frequent factors. We instantiate this with a sparse autoencoder trained on the target dataset, using sparse unit activations to summarize both individual samples and dataset-wide factor statistics. Second, we realize process-level diversity by combining rare-factor sampling with a Usage-Frequency Penalty that promotes sample rotation, provably discourages monopoly, and reduces gradient bias. Third, we couple the two-dimensional scoring with a smooth scheduler that transitions selection from core-pattern consolidation to rare-factor exploration, without extra gradients, influence estimates, or second-order computations on the training model. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks across vision and text tasks demonstrate improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across models. Our method matches or exceeds full-data accuracy with over 2x training acceleration. Code will be released.

</details>


### [159] [S5-SHB Agent: Society 5.0 enabled Multi-model Agentic Blockchain Framework for Smart Home](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05027)
*Janani Rangila,Akila Siriweera,Incheon Paik,Keitaro Naruse,Isuru Jayanada,Vishmika Devindi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S5-SHB-Agent：面向Society 5.0的区块链智能家居多智能体框架，通过自适应共识、十智能体协调和四层治理模型解决现有系统的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链智能家居框架存在三大局限：1）依赖固定共识协议的刚性智能合约；2）最多使用单一AI模型，缺乏多智能体协调；3）没有让居民控制自动化行为的治理机制。这些局限阻碍了实现Society 5.0愿景中以人为本的智能家居自治系统。

Method: 提出S5-SHB-Agent框架：1）使用十个专用智能体，通过可互换的大语言模型在安全、安防、舒适、能源、隐私和健康领域做出决策；2）自适应PoW区块链根据交易量和紧急情况调整挖矿难度，采用数字签名和Merkle树确保防篡改可审计性；3）四层治理模型让居民通过分层偏好控制自动化，从日常调整到不可变的安全阈值。

Result: 评估证实：1）居民治理在所有测试配置中正确分离了可调整的舒适优先级和不可变的安全阈值；2）自适应共识机制成功提交紧急区块。

Conclusion: S5-SHB-Agent框架通过自适应共识、多智能体协调和分层治理模型，为区块链治理的智能家居提供了符合Society 5.0原则的解决方案，实现了透明、可审计且居民可控的自动化决策。

Abstract: The smart home is a key application domain within the Society 5.0 vision for a human-centered society. As smart home ecosystems expand with heterogeneous IoT protocols, diverse devices, and evolving threats, autonomous systems must manage comfort, security, energy, and safety for residents. Such autonomous decision-making requires a trust anchor, making blockchain a preferred foundation for transparent and accountable smart home governance. However, realizing this vision requires blockchain-governed smart homes to simultaneously address adaptive consensus, intelligent multi-agent coordination, and resident-controlled governance aligned with the principles of Society 5.0. Existing frameworks rely solely on rigid smart contracts with fixed consensus protocols, employ at most a single AI model without multi-agent coordination, and offer no governance mechanism for residents to control automation behaviour. To address these limitations, this paper presents the Society 5.0-driven human-centered governance-enabled smart home blockchain agent (S5-SHB-Agent). The framework orchestrates ten specialized agents using interchangeable large language models to make decisions across the safety, security, comfort, energy, privacy, and health domains. An adaptive PoW blockchain adjusts mining difficulty based on transaction volume and emergency conditions, with digital signatures and Merkle tree anchoring to ensure tamper evident auditability. A four-tier governance model enables residents to control automation through tiered preferences from routine adjustments to immutable safety thresholds. Evaluation confirms that resident governance correctly separates adjustable comfort priorities from immutable safety thresholds across all tested configurations, while adaptive consensus commits emergency blocks.

</details>


### [160] [AegisUI: Behavioral Anomaly Detection for Structured User Interface Protocols in AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05031)
*Mohd Safwan Uddin,Saba Hajira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AegisUI框架研究UI payload中的行为不匹配攻击，生成结构化UI payload并注入攻击，提取18个特征，比较三种异常检测器，Random Forest表现最佳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前防御系统仅检查payload语法，无法检测UI组件行为与用户预期不匹配的攻击（如按钮标签与实际操作不符），存在安全漏洞

Method: 构建AegisUI框架：生成结构化UI payload，注入五种攻击类型（钓鱼界面、数据泄露、布局滥用、操纵性UI、工作流异常），提取18个结构/语义/绑定/会话特征，比较三种检测器（Isolation Forest、良性训练自编码器、Random Forest）

Result: Random Forest表现最佳（准确率0.931，精确率0.980，召回率0.740，F1 0.843，ROC-AUC 0.952）；自编码器次之（F1 0.762，ROC-AUC 0.863）且无需恶意标签；布局滥用最易检测，操纵性UI最难检测

Conclusion: AegisUI填补了UI payload行为安全检测的空白，提供可复现的基准测试框架，Random Forest在监督学习中表现最优，自编码器在缺乏攻击历史的新系统中具有实用价值

Abstract: AI agents that build user interfaces on the fly assembling buttons, forms, and data displays from structured protocol payloads are becoming common in production systems. The trouble is that a payload can pass every schema check and still trick a user: a button might say "View invoice" while its hidden action wipes an account, or a display widget might quietly bind to an internal salary field. Current defenses stop at syntax; they were never built to catch this kind of behavioral mismatch.
  We built AegisUI to study exactly this gap. The framework generates structured UI payloads, injects realistic attacks into them, extracts numeric features, and benchmarks anomaly detectors end-to-end. We produced 4000 labeled payloads (3000 benign, 1000 malicious) spanning five application domains and five attack families: phishing interfaces, data leakage, layout abuse, manipulative UI, and workflow anomalies.
  From each payload we extracted 18 features covering structural, semantic, binding, and session dimensions, then compared three detectors: Isolation Forest (unsupervised), a benign-trained autoencoder (semi-supervised), and Random Forest (supervised). On a stratified 80/20 split, Random Forest scored best overall (accuracy 0.931, precision 0.980, recall 0.740, F1 0.843, ROC-AUC 0.952). The autoencoder came second (F1 0.762, ROC-AUC 0.863) and needs no malicious labels at training time, which matters when deploying a new system that lacks attack history. Per-attack-type analysis showed that layout abuse is easiest to catch while manipulative UI payloads are hardest. All code, data, and configurations are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [161] [The Trilingual Triad Framework: Integrating Design, AI, and Domain Knowledge in No-code AI Smart City Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05036)
*Qian Huang,King Wang Poon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"三语三元组"框架，解释学生如何通过整合设计、AI和领域知识来学习使用生成式AI进行设计，帮助学生从被动使用AI输出转变为主动设计AI协作伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI快速进入高等教育，学生通常只是被动使用AI生成输出，而非主动创建AI驱动的知识工具。研究旨在探索学生如何从使用AI工具转变为设计AI协作伙伴，促进更有效的人机协作。

Method: 采用定性多案例研究方法，分析新加坡科技设计大学研究生课程"创建无代码智慧城市前沿"中的三个项目：Interview Companion GPT、Urban Observer GPT和Buddy Buddy。从设计、AI架构和领域专业知识三个维度进行分析。

Result: 研究发现，当设计、AI和领域知识这三种"语言"协同工作时，有效的人机协作就会出现：领域知识构建AI逻辑，设计调解人机交互，AI扩展学习者的认知能力。

Conclusion: 三语三元组框架表明，构建AI系统可以作为建构主义学习过程，增强AI素养、元认知和学习者能动性，帮助学生从AI使用者转变为AI设计者。

Abstract: This paper introduces the "Trilingual Triad" framework, a model that explains how students learn to design with generative artificial intelligence (AI) through the integration of Design, AI, and Domain Knowledge. As generative AI rapidly enters higher education, students often engage with these systems as passive users of generated outputs rather than active creators of AI-enabled knowledge tools. This study investigates how students can transition from using AI as a tool to designing AI as a collaborative teammate. The research examines a graduate course, Creating the Frontier of No-code Smart Cities at the Singapore University of Technology and Design (SUTD), in which students developed domain-specific custom GPT systems without coding. Using a qualitative multi-case study approach, three projects - the Interview Companion GPT, the Urban Observer GPT, and Buddy Buddy - were analyzed across three dimensions: design, AI architecture, and domain expertise. The findings show that effective human-AI collaboration emerges when these three "languages" are orchestrated together: domain knowledge structures the AI's logic, design mediates human-AI interaction, and AI extends learners' cognitive capacity. The Trilingual Triad framework highlights how building AI systems can serve as a constructionist learning process that strengthens AI literacy, metacognition, and learner agency.

</details>


### [162] [Enhancing Zero-shot Commonsense Reasoning by Integrating Visual Knowledge via Machine Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05040)
*Hyuntae Park,Yeachan Kim,SangKeun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Imagine框架通过机器生成的图像增强预训练语言模型的常识推理能力，在零样本设置下显著超越现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本常识推理模型受限于文本知识中的人类报告偏差，导致机器与人类理解存在差异，需要引入多模态信息来弥补这一差距

Method: 提出Imagine框架，将图像生成器嵌入推理流程，为文本输入补充机器生成的视觉信号；构建合成数据集模拟视觉问答场景，促进视觉上下文的有效利用

Result: 在多个常识推理基准测试中，Imagine显著优于现有零样本方法，甚至超越先进的大型语言模型

Conclusion: 机器想象能够有效缓解报告偏差，显著提升常识推理模型的泛化能力，证明了多模态增强在零样本常识推理中的价值

Abstract: Recent advancements in zero-shot commonsense reasoning have empowered Pre-trained Language Models (PLMs) to acquire extensive commonsense knowledge without requiring task-specific fine-tuning. Despite this progress, these models frequently suffer from limitations caused by human reporting biases inherent in textual knowledge, leading to understanding discrepancies between machines and humans. To bridge this gap, we introduce an additional modality to enrich the reasoning capabilities of PLMs. We propose Imagine (Machine Imagination-based Reasoning), a novel zero-shot commonsense reasoning framework that supplements textual inputs with visual signals from machine-generated images. Specifically, we enhance PLMs with the ability to imagine by embedding an image generator directly into the reasoning pipeline. To facilitate effective utilization of this imagined visual context, we construct synthetic datasets designed to emulate visual question-answering scenarios. Through comprehensive evaluations on multiple commonsense reasoning benchmarks, we demonstrate that Imagine substantially outperforms existing zero-shot approaches and even surpasses advanced large language models. These results underscore the capability of machine imagination to mitigate reporting bias and significantly enhance the generalization ability of commonsense reasoning models

</details>


### [163] [Bidirectional Curriculum Generation: A Multi-Agent Framework for Data-Efficient Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05120)
*Boren Hu,Xiao Liu,Boci Peng,Xinping Zhao,Xiaoran Shang,Yun Zhu,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双向课程生成框架，通过多智能体生态系统动态调整问题复杂度，优化数据利用效率，显著提升数学推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的数学推理能力提升通常需要海量数据集，但数据效率仍是关键瓶颈。传统单向课程学习（简单到复杂）存在样本利用效率低的问题，盲目增加复杂度而忽略基础缺陷，导致计算资源浪费在无法解决的问题上。

Method: 提出双向课程生成框架，采用多智能体生态系统模拟自适应教学法，建立闭环反馈循环。动态生成数据：通过复杂化问题挑战模型，或简化问题修复特定推理缺陷。基于最优步调定理优化学习轨迹。

Result: 该方法显著优于基线模型，在显著减少指令样本数量的同时实现了更优的推理性能。

Conclusion: 双向课程生成框架通过动态调整问题复杂度，确保模型在任何阶段都只消费最有效的数据，从而最大化每个训练样本的教学价值，解决了数据效率瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Enhancing mathematical reasoning in Large Language Models typically demands massive datasets, yet data efficiency remains a critical bottleneck. While Curriculum Learning attempts to structure this process, standard unidirectional approaches (simple-to-complex) suffer from inefficient sample utilization: they blindly escalate complexity even when foundational gaps persist, leading to wasted computation on unsolvable problems. To maximize the instructional value of every training sample, we introduce a novel Bidirectional Curriculum Generation framework. Unlike rigid trajectories, our multi-agent ecosystem mimics adaptive pedagogy to establish a closed feedback loop. It dynamically generates data by either complicating problems to challenge the model or, crucially, simplying them to repair specific reasoning failures. This mechanism ensures that the model consumes only the most effective data at any given stage. Grounded in the Optimal Pacing Theorem, our approach optimizes the learning trajectory, significantly outperforming baselines while achieving superior reasoning performance with substantially fewer instruction samples.

</details>


### [164] [MedCoRAG: Interpretable Hepatology Diagnosis via Hybrid Evidence Retrieval and Multispecialty Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05129)
*Zheng Li,Jiayi Xu,Zhikai Hu,Hechang Chen,Lele Cong,Yunyun Wang,Shuchao Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedCoRAG是一个用于肝病诊断的端到端框架，通过多智能体协作检索增强生成，结合知识图谱和临床指南，实现可解释的诊断推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI临床诊断方法缺乏透明度、结构化推理和可部署性，且通常从单一来源检索证据，不支持基于结构化临床数据的迭代、角色专业化审议。

Method: 提出MedCoRAG框架：1)从标准化异常发现生成诊断假设；2)联合检索和剪枝UMLS知识图谱路径和临床指南构建患者特异性证据包；3)多智能体协作推理：路由器智能体根据病例复杂度动态调度专家智能体，专家智能体迭代推理证据并在需要时触发针对性重新检索，通用智能体将所有审议合成为可追溯的共识诊断。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV肝病病例上的实验结果表明，MedCoRAG在诊断性能和推理可解释性方面均优于现有方法和闭源模型。

Conclusion: MedCoRAG通过多智能体协作检索增强生成，结合结构化临床数据和专业知识，实现了更准确、可解释的肝病诊断，模拟了多学科会诊过程。

Abstract: Diagnosing hepatic diseases accurately and interpretably is critical, yet it remains challenging in real-world clinical settings. Existing AI approaches for clinical diagnosis often lack transparency, structured reasoning, and deployability. Recent efforts have leveraged large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent collaboration. However, these approaches typically retrieve evidence from a single source and fail to support iterative, role-specialized deliberation grounded in structured clinical data. To address this, we propose MedCoRAG (i.e., Medical Collaborative RAG), an end-to-end framework that generates diagnostic hypotheses from standardized abnormal findings and constructs a patient-specific evidence package by jointly retrieving and pruning UMLS knowledge graph paths and clinical guidelines. It then performs Multi-Agent Collaborative Reasoning: a Router Agent dynamically dispatches Specialist Agents based on case complexity; these agents iteratively reason over the evidence and trigger targeted re-retrievals when needed, while a Generalist Agent synthesizes all deliberations into a traceable consensus diagnosis that emulates multidisciplinary consultation. Experimental results on hepatic disease cases from MIMIC-IV show that MedCoRAG outperforms existing methods and closed-source models in both diagnostic performance and reasoning interpretability.

</details>


### [165] [X-RAY: Mapping LLM Reasoning Capability via Formalized and Calibrated Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05290)
*Gao Tianxi,Cai Yufan,Yuan Yusi,Dong Jin Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-RAY是一个可解释的推理分析系统，通过形式化校准的探针评估LLM的推理能力，揭示LLM在约束精化和解空间重构上的不对称表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注任务级准确率，往往混淆模式匹配与推理能力，缺乏对LLM推理能力的深入理解。需要开发能够精确分析推理能力的方法。

Method: 提出X-RAY系统，将推理能力建模为可提取结构的功能，通过约束交互、推理深度和解空间几何等形式属性进行操作化。使用形式化工具生成具有受控结构变化的探针，通过形式校准和验证精确分离增量结构信息。

Result: 评估显示LLM推理存在系统性不对称：模型对约束精化相对稳健（额外条件缩小现有解空间），但在解空间重构下急剧退化（修改改变解流形的基本结构形式）。校准的形式探针能区分标准基准上表现相似的模型，并揭示结构可解释而非不透明的失败模式。

Conclusion: X-RAY提供了一个无污染、可解释的推理分析框架，不仅能评估LLM推理能力，还能支持推理模型的训练和测试，为理解LLM推理机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve promising performance, yet their ability to reason remains poorly understood. Existing evaluations largely emphasize task-level accuracy, often conflating pattern matching with reasoning capability. We present X-RAY, an explainable reasoning analysis system that maps the LLM reasoning capability using calibrated, formally verified probes. We model reasoning capability as a function of extractable \textit{structure}, operationalized through formal properties such as constraint interaction, reasoning depth, and solution-space geometry. X-Ray generates probes via formal tools with controlled structural variations, enabling precise isolation of incremental structural information through formal calibration and verification. We evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs on problems ranging from junior-level to advanced in mathematics, physics, and chemistry. Our analysis reveals a systematic asymmetry in LLM reasoning: models are relatively robust to constraint refinement, where additional conditions shrink an existing solution space, but degrade sharply under solution-space restructuring, where modifications alter the underlying structural form of the solution manifold. Moreover, calibrated formal probes differentiate models that appear indistinguishable on standard benchmarks and reveal failure modes that are structurally interpretable rather than opaque. Beyond evaluation, our framework is contamination-free and supports the training and testing of reasoning models.

</details>


### [166] [STRUCTUREDAGENT: Planning with AND/OR Trees for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05294)
*ELita Lobo,Xu Chen,Jingjing Meng,Nan Xi,Yang Jiao,Chirag Agarwal,Yair Zick,Yan Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STRUCTUREDAGENT是一个用于复杂长视野网页浏览任务的分层规划框架，通过动态AND/OR树和结构化内存提升LLM智能体性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体在复杂长视野任务上表现不佳，主要受限于上下文内存有限、规划能力弱和贪婪行为导致过早终止等问题

Method: 提出分层规划框架，包含两个核心组件：1)使用动态AND/OR树进行高效搜索的在线分层规划器；2)跟踪和维护候选解决方案的结构化内存模块

Result: 在WebVoyager、WebArena和自定义购物基准测试中，STRUCTUREDAGENT相比标准LLM智能体在长视野网页浏览任务上性能显著提升

Conclusion: STRUCTUREDAGENT通过分层规划和结构化内存有效解决了现有网页智能体的局限性，同时提供可解释的层次化计划便于调试和人工干预

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic systems for sequential decision-making. Such agents must perceive their environment, reason across multiple time steps, and take actions that optimize long-term objectives. However, existing web agents struggle on complex, long-horizon tasks due to limited in-context memory for tracking history, weak planning abilities, and greedy behaviors that lead to premature termination. To address these challenges, we propose STRUCTUREDAGENT, a hierarchical planning framework with two core components: (1) an online hierarchical planner that uses dynamic AND/OR trees for efficient search and (2) a structured memory module that tracks and maintains candidate solutions to improve constraint satisfaction in information-seeking tasks. The framework also produces interpretable hierarchical plans, enabling easier debugging and facilitating human intervention when needed. Our results on WebVoyager, WebArena, and custom shopping benchmarks show that STRUCTUREDAGENT improves performance on long-horizon web-browsing tasks compared to standard LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [167] [WebChain: A Large-Scale Human-Annotated Dataset of Real-World Web Interaction Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05295)
*Sicheng Fan,Rui Wan,Yifei Leng,Gaoning Liang,Li Ling,Yanyi Shang,Dehan Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebChain是最大的开源网页交互轨迹数据集，包含31,725条轨迹和318k步骤，通过三重对齐提供多模态监督，支持网页智能体研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体研究缺乏大规模、高质量的真实世界交互数据，合成方法难以覆盖复杂高价值任务，需要可复现的研究基础。

Method: 构建WebChain数据集：通过可扩展的采集流程收集真实网站上的交互轨迹，实现视觉、结构和动作数据的三重对齐。提出双重中间训练方法，将空间定位与规划解耦。

Result: WebChain包含31,725条轨迹和318k步骤，是目前最大的开源网页交互数据集。基于该数据集的双重中间训练方法在WebChainBench和其他公开GUI基准测试中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: WebChain为构建和严格评估下一代可扩展网页智能体提供了必要的数据和洞见，将加速网页智能体研究的可复现性。

Abstract: We introduce WebChain, the largest open-source dataset of human-annotated trajectories on real-world websites, designed to accelerate reproducible research in web agents. It contains 31,725 trajectories and 318k steps, featuring a core Triple Alignment of visual, structural, and action data to provide rich, multi-modal supervision. The data is collected via a scalable pipeline that ensures coverage of complex, high-value tasks often missed by synthetic methods. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a Dual Mid-Training recipe that decouples spatial grounding from planning, achieving state-of-the-art performance on our proposed WebChainBench and other public GUI benchmarks. Our work provides the data and insights necessary to build and rigorously evaluate the next generation of scalable web agents.

</details>


### [168] [Ailed: A Psyche-Driven Chess Engine with Dynamic Emotional Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05352)
*Diego Armando Resendez Prado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于人格-心理分解的象棋引擎行为变异框架，通过音频信号链动态调整走子概率分布，模拟人类棋手的心理波动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统象棋引擎虽然实力超越人类，但缺乏人类棋手在时间压力、心理波动下的行为特征。现有引擎无法模拟人类棋手在压力下的失误、过度自信等心理状态变化，需要一种能够产生行为变异性的框架。

Method: 提出人格-心理分解框架：人格是静态预设，心理是动态标量ψ_t∈[-100,+100]，每步棋后根据五个位置因素重新计算。两者输入音频信号链（噪声门、压缩器/扩展器、五段均衡器、饱和限制器），实时重塑走子概率分布。该框架独立于底层引擎，适用于任何输出走子概率的系统。

Result: 在12,414局对Maia2-1100的测试中，使用两种训练数据相差约2800倍的概率源，均显示相同单调梯度：从压力到过度自信，最佳走子一致性有20-25个百分点的差异。过度自信时与原始Maia2一致性达66%，压力下竞争得分从50.8%降至30.1%。行为模式类似人类棋手的"倾斜"和过度自信。

Conclusion: 人格-心理分解框架成功产生了可预测的行为变异性，行为变异来自信号链而非底层模型。该框架能够模拟人类棋手的心理波动特征，但缺乏人类主体验证。为象棋引擎引入人类行为特征提供了新方法。

Abstract: Chess engines passed human strength years ago, but they still don't play like humans. A grandmaster under clock pressure blunders in ways a club player on a hot streak never would. Conventional engines capture none of this.
  This paper proposes a personality x psyche decomposition to produce behavioral variability in chess play, drawing on patterns observed in human games. Personality is static -- a preset that pins down the engine's character. Psyche is dynamic -- a bounded scalar ψ_t \in [-100, +100], recomputed from five positional factors after every move. These two components feed into an audio-inspired signal chain (noise gate, compressor/expander, five-band equalizer, saturation limiter) that reshapes move probability distributions on the fly. The chain doesn't care what engine sits behind it: any system that outputs move probabilities will do. It needs no search and carries no state beyond ψ_t.
  I test the framework across 12,414 games against Maia2-1100, feeding it two probability sources that differ by ~2,800x in training data. Both show the same monotonic gradient in top-move agreement (~20-25 pp spread from stress to overconfidence), which tells us the behavioral variation comes from the signal chain, not from the model underneath. When the psyche runs overconfident, the chain mostly gets out of the way (66% agreement with vanilla Maia2). Under stress, the competitive score falls from 50.8% to 30.1%. The patterns are reminiscent of tilt and overconfidence as described in human play, but I should be upfront: this study includes no human-subject validation.

</details>


### [169] [PACE: A Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine for 9-1-1 Call-taker Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05361)
*Zirong Chen,Hongchao Zhang,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE系统通过个性化自适应课程引擎，利用概率技能状态建模和情境多臂老虎机算法，为911调度员培训提供个性化场景推荐，显著提升培训效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 911调度员培训需要掌握上千项相互依赖的技能，涵盖多种事件类型和协议细节。全国性劳动力短缺已使培训能力紧张，而有效的教学仍要求培训师根据每位学员不断发展的能力来定制目标。这种个性化负担是当前实践无法扩展的。

Method: PACE系统通过三个核心功能增强培训师决策：(1)维护学员技能状态的概率信念，(2)建模个体学习和遗忘动态，(3)推荐平衡新能力获取与现有能力保持的训练场景。系统在结构化技能图上传播证据以加速诊断覆盖，并应用情境多臂老虎机选择针对学员准备解决的技能差距的场景。

Result: 实证结果显示，PACE相比最先进框架实现了19.50%更快的达到能力时间，以及10.95%更高的最终掌握水平。与在职培训官员的协同研究表明，在真实案例中PACE与专家教学判断的一致性率达到95.45%。在评估方面，PACE将周转时间从11.58分钟减少到仅34秒，减少了高达95.08%。

Conclusion: PACE系统通过个性化自适应课程引擎，有效解决了911调度员培训中的个性化扩展问题，显著提升了培训效率和效果，为紧急通信培训提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: 9-1-1 call-taking training requires mastery of over a thousand interdependent skills, covering diverse incident types and protocol-specific nuances. A nationwide labor shortage is already straining training capacity, but effective instruction still demands that trainers tailor objectives to each trainee's evolving competencies. This personalization burden is one that current practice cannot scale. Partnering with Metro Nashville Department of Emergency Communications (MNDEC), we propose PACE (Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine), a co-pilot system that augments trainer decision-making by (1) maintaining probabilistic beliefs over trainee skill states, (2) modeling individual learning and forgetting dynamics, and (3) recommending training scenarios that balance acquisition of new competencies with retention of existing ones. PACE propagates evidence over a structured skill graph to accelerate diagnostic coverage and applies contextual bandits to select scenarios that target gaps the trainee is prepared to address. Empirical results show that PACE achieves 19.50% faster time-to-competence and 10.95% higher terminal mastery compared to state-of-the-art frameworks. Co-pilot studies with practicing training officers further demonstrate a 95.45% alignment rate between PACE's and experts' pedagogical judgments on real-world cases. Under estimation, PACE cuts turnaround time to merely 34 seconds from 11.58 minutes, up to 95.08% reduction.

</details>


### [170] [Legal interpretation and AI: from expert systems to argumentation and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05392)
*Václav Janeček,Giovanni Sartor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI与法律研究中法律解释的三种主要方法：专家系统注重法律知识工程，论证研究关注解释性论证的结构与互动，机器学习研究专注于通过语言模型自动生成解释建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在法律领域应用的不断演进，法律解释作为核心问题需要系统梳理不同研究方法的特点与贡献。本文旨在分析AI与法律研究中处理法律解释问题的三种主要路径，揭示各自的方法论演变和实际应用价值。

Method: 采用文献综述和分类分析方法，将AI与法律研究中的法律解释方法分为三类：1)专家系统方法，侧重于法律知识工程和精确知识转移；2)论证方法，关注解释性论证的结构化表示和辩证互动；3)机器学习方法，利用通用和专用语言模型自动生成解释建议和论证。

Result: 分析表明三种方法各有侧重：专家系统确保解释的一致性应用，论证方法评估解释性主张的可接受性，机器学习方法通过语言模型生成解释建议并已在法律实践中得到部署。这些方法共同构成了AI处理法律解释问题的完整框架。

Conclusion: AI与法律研究通过不同方法处理法律解释问题，从早期的知识工程到现代的机器学习应用，形成了互补的研究范式。未来研究需要整合这些方法，以应对法律解释的复杂性和实践需求，推动AI在法律领域的深入应用。

Abstract: AI and Law research has encountered legal interpretation in different ways, in the context of its evolving approaches and methodologies. Research on expert system has focused on legal knowledge engineering, with the goal of ensuring that human-generated interpretations can be precisely transferred into knowledge-bases, to be consistently applied. Research on argumentation has aimed at representing the structure of interpretive arguments, as well as their dialectical interactions, to assess of the acceptability of interpretive claims within argumentation frameworks. Research on machine learning has focused on the automated generation of interpretive suggestions and arguments, through general and specialised language models, now being increasingly deployed in legal practice.

</details>


### [171] [Dissociating Direct Access from Inference in AI Introspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05414)
*Harvey Lederman,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了AI模型的内省机制，发现模型通过概率匹配和直接访问内部状态两种可分离机制检测注入表征，其中直接访问机制是内容无关的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内省是基础认知能力但其机制尚不明确，虽然已有研究表明AI模型能够内省，但对其具体机制的理解仍然有限，需要深入研究AI模型的内省工作机制。

Method: 首先广泛复现Lindsey等人(2025)的思想注入检测范式，在大型开源模型中研究内省机制，分析模型如何检测注入表征，区分概率匹配和直接访问内部状态两种机制。

Result: 模型通过两种可分离机制检测注入表征：(1)概率匹配(从感知到的提示异常推断)；(2)直接访问内部状态。直接访问机制是内容无关的：模型能检测到异常发生但无法可靠识别其语义内容。研究的模型类别会虚构高频且具体的注入概念(如"苹果")，正确概念猜测通常需要显著更多的token。

Conclusion: 这种内容无关的内省机制与哲学和心理学中的主要理论相一致，为理解AI模型的内省工作机制提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Introspection is a foundational cognitive ability, but its mechanism is not well understood. Recent work has shown that AI models can introspect. We study their mechanism of introspection, first extensively replicating Lindsey et al. (2025)'s thought injection detection paradigm in large open-source models. We show that these models detect injected representations via two separable mechanisms: (i) probability-matching (inferring from perceived anomaly of the prompt) and (ii) direct access to internal states. The direct access mechanism is content-agnostic: models detect that an anomaly occurred but cannot reliably identify its semantic content. The two model classes we study confabulate injected concepts that are high-frequency and concrete (e.g., "apple'"); for them correct concept guesses typically require significantly more tokens. This content-agnostic introspective mechanism is consistent with leading theories in philosophy and psychology.

</details>


### [172] [Distributed Partial Information Puzzles: Examining Common Ground Construction Under Epistemic Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05450)
*Yifan Zhu,Mariah Bradford,Kenneth Lai,Timothy Obiso,Videep Venkatesha,James Pustejovsky,Nikhil Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出分布式部分信息拼图（DPIP）任务来研究多模态多方协作中的共同基础建立，创建了带标注的多模态数据集，并比较了LLM和基于动态认知逻辑的两种建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在多模态多方协作中建立共同基础（共享信念和相互认可的事实）面临挑战，尤其是在参与者拥有不同信息的认知不对称情况下。

Method: 1. 引入分布式部分信息拼图（DPIP）协作构建任务，引发认知不对称下的丰富多模态通信；2. 创建多模态交互数据集，在语音、手势和动作模态上进行标注和时间对齐；3. 评估两种共同基础建模范式：基于提示的大型语言模型和基于动态认知逻辑的公理化流水线。

Result: DPIP任务对现代LLM同时追踪任务进展和信念状态的能力构成挑战，表明当前模型在多模态多方协作中建立共同基础的能力有限。

Conclusion: DPIP任务为研究多模态多方协作中的共同基础建立提供了有效测试平台，基于动态认知逻辑的方法在追踪信念动态方面可能比纯LLM方法更具优势，为开发更有效的协作AI系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Establishing common ground, a shared set of beliefs and mutually recognized facts, is fundamental to collaboration, yet remains a challenge for current AI systems, especially in multimodal, multiparty settings, where the collaborators bring different information to the table. We introduce the Distributed Partial Information Puzzle (DPIP), a collaborative construction task that elicits rich multimodal communication under epistemic asymmetry. We present a multimodal dataset of these interactions, annotated and temporally aligned across speech, gesture, and action modalities to support reasoning over propositional content and belief dynamics. We then evaluate two paradigms for modeling common ground (CG): (1) state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), prompted to infer shared beliefs from multimodal updates, and (2) an axiomatic pipeline grounded in Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) that incrementally performs the same task. Results on the annotated DPIP data indicate that it poses a challenge to modern LLMs' abilities to track both task progression and belief state.

</details>


### [173] [Towards Provably Unbiased LLM Judges via Bias-Bounded Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05485)
*Benjamin Feuer,Lucas Rosenblatt,Oussama Elachqar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出平均偏置有界性(A-BB)框架，为LLM评委提供形式化保证，减少可测量偏置带来的危害，在Arena-Hard-Auto数据集上验证效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统向自主反馈循环发展，需要自动可验证的奖励机制。在缺乏确定性基准的场景中，LLM作为评委成为实用方案，但现有系统缺乏对未知或对抗性偏置的强保证机制

Method: 提出平均偏置有界性(A-BB)算法框架，形式化保证减少LLM评委中任何可测量偏置带来的危害/影响。在Arena-Hard-Auto数据集上使用四个LLM评委进行验证

Result: 在格式化和模式偏置设置下，实现了(tau=0.5, delta=0.01)的偏置有界保证，同时保持61-99%与原始排名的相关性，大多数评委-偏置组合超过80%相关性

Conclusion: A-BB框架为LLM评委系统提供了对抗未知或对抗性偏置的形式化保证，在保持排名质量的同时显著降低偏置影响，推进了自主AI系统中可靠奖励机制的发展

Abstract: As AI models progress beyond simple chatbots into more complex workflows, we draw ever closer to the event horizon beyond which AI systems will be utilized in autonomous, self-maintaining feedback loops. Any autonomous AI system will depend on automated, verifiable rewards and feedback; in settings where ground truth is sparse or non-deterministic, one practical source of such rewards is an LLM-as-a-Judge. Although LLM judges continue to improve, the literature has yet to introduce systems capable of enforcing standards with strong guarantees, particularly when bias vectors are unknown or adversarially discovered. To remedy this issue, we propose average bias-boundedness (A-BB), an algorithmic framework which formally guarantees reductions of harm/impact as a result of any measurable bias in an LLM judge. Evaluating on Arena-Hard-Auto with four LLM judges, we achieve (tau=0.5, delta=0.01) bias-bounded guarantees while retaining 61-99% correlation with original rankings across formatting and schematic bias settings, with most judge-bias combinations exceeding 80%. The code to reproduce our findings is available at https://github.com/penfever/bias-bounded-evaluation.

</details>


### [174] [The Spike, the Sparse and the Sink: Anatomy of Massive Activations and Attention Sinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05498)
*Shangwen Sun,Alfredo Canziani,Yann LeCun,Jiachen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer语言模型中的大规模激活和注意力汇是两个相关但功能不同的现象，它们的共现主要是现代Transformer架构设计的人工产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型中存在两个反复出现的现象：大规模激活（少数token在少数通道中表现出极端异常值）和注意力汇（某些token无论语义相关性如何都吸引不成比例的注意力）。先前工作观察到这些现象经常共现且涉及相同的token，但它们的功能作用和因果关系尚不清楚。

Method: 通过系统实验，研究这两个现象的功能角色和关系。识别预归一化配置作为实现共现的关键设计选择，并通过消融实验验证。

Result: 大规模激活和注意力汇服务于相关但不同的功能：大规模激活全局运作，诱导跨层持久的近似恒定隐藏表示，作为模型的隐式参数；注意力汇局部运作，调节注意力输出并偏向短程依赖。预归一化配置是共现的关键，消融它会导致两个现象解耦。

Conclusion: Transformer语言模型中大规模激活和注意力汇的共现主要是架构设计的人工产物，而非功能必然性。这两个现象具有不同的功能角色：大规模激活作为全局隐式参数，注意力汇作为局部注意力调节器。预归一化配置是实现这种共现的关键架构特征。

Abstract: We study two recurring phenomena in Transformer language models: massive activations, in which a small number of tokens exhibit extreme outliers in a few channels, and attention sinks, in which certain tokens attract disproportionate attention mass regardless of semantic relevance. Prior work observes that these phenomena frequently co-occur and often involve the same tokens, but their functional roles and causal relationship remain unclear. Through systematic experiments, we show that the co-occurrence is largely an architectural artifact of modern Transformer design, and that the two phenomena serve related but distinct functions. Massive activations operate globally: they induce near-constant hidden representations that persist across layers, effectively functioning as implicit parameters of the model. Attention sinks operate locally: they modulate attention outputs across heads and bias individual heads toward short-range dependencies. We identify the pre-norm configuration as the key choice that enables the co-occurrence, and show that ablating it causes the two phenomena to decouple.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Multistage Stochastic Programming for Rare Event Risk Mitigation in Power Systems Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04734)
*Daniel Mastropietro,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多阶段场景优化的罕见事件感知电力系统控制方法，使用Fleming-Viot粒子方法偏向生成极低风电的罕见场景，以获得在可再生能源长期短缺下具有鲁棒性的经济有效控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高比例间歇性可再生能源渗透给电力系统运行管理带来鲁棒性挑战。当天气分布的尾部实现导致长时间太阳能辐照和风速不足时，需要提前启动传统发电机组。这种事件触发成本高昂，不准确的预测既会造成浪费也可能导致灾难性供电不足，因此需要特别关注噪声建模和动态特性

Method: 采用多阶段场景优化方法进行罕见事件感知控制。使用Fleming-Viot粒子方法偏向生成极低风电功率的罕见场景，通过这种偏向的场景生成来获得成本有效的传统发电机组控制策略

Result: 该方法能够生成针对可再生能源长期短缺的鲁棒控制策略，在罕见天气事件发生时提供经济有效的传统发电机组调度方案

Conclusion: 提出的罕见事件感知控制方法能够有效应对高比例可再生能源系统中的极端天气事件，通过偏向罕见场景的优化提高了电力系统在可再生能源长期短缺情况下的鲁棒性和经济性

Abstract: High intermittent renewable penetration in the energy mix presents challenges in robustness for the management of power systems' operation. If a tail realization of the distribution of weather yields a prolonged period of time during which solar irradiation and wind speed are insufficient for satisfying energy demand, then it becomes critical to ramp up the generation of conventional power plants with adequate foresight. This event trigger is costly, and inaccurate forecasting can either be wasteful or yield catastrophic undersupply. This encourages particular attention to accurate modeling of the noise and the resulting dynamics within the aforementioned scenario. In this work we present a method for rare event-aware control of power systems using multi-stage scenario-based optimization. A Fleming-Viot particle approach is used to bias the scenario generation towards rare realizations of very low wind power, in order to obtain a cost-effective control of conventional power plants that is robust under prolonged renewable energy shortfalls.

</details>


### [176] [Policy Optimization of Mixed H2/H-infinity Control: Benign Nonconvexity and Global Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04843)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yuto Watanabe,Yujie Tang,Yang Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文从现代策略优化视角重新审视混合H2/H∞控制，揭示了其良性非凸结构：所有驻点都是全局最优，并提出了扩展凸提升框架来连接非凸策略优化与凸重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Riccati/LMI方法对混合H2/H∞控制的非凸优化景观提供有限洞察，且难以扩展到大规模或数据驱动场景。需要从现代策略优化视角重新审视该问题，以揭示其内在结构并设计可扩展算法。

Method: 采用策略优化视角分析混合H2/H∞控制，包括一般双通道和单通道情况。提出扩展凸提升（ECL）框架，通过非严格Riccati不等式连接非凸策略优化与凸重构，并推导显式梯度公式。

Result: 证明了混合H2/H∞控制具有良性非凸结构：所有驻点都是全局最优。刻画了H∞约束可行集为开集、路径连通集，边界由满足H∞约束的策略给出。混合目标函数在内部是实解析的，并给出了显式梯度公式。

Conclusion: 混合H2/H∞控制存在隐藏的凸性结构，扩展凸提升框架揭示了这一特性，为设计大规模场景下的可扩展策略迭代方法提供了理论基础，使混合控制问题更易于分析和求解。

Abstract: Mixed H2/H-infinity control balances performance and robustness by minimizing an H2 cost bound subject to an H-infinity constraint. However, classical Riccati/LMI solutions offer limited insight into the nonconvex optimization landscape and do not readily scale to large-scale or data-driven settings. In this paper, we revisit mixed H2/H-infinity control from a modern policy optimization viewpoint, including the general two-channel and single-channel cases. One central result is that both cases enjoy a benign nonconvex structure: every stationary point is globally optimal. We characterize the H-infinity-constrained feasible set, which is open, path-connected, with boundary given exactly by policies saturating the H-infinity constraint. We also show that the mixed objective is real analytic in the interior with explicit gradient formulas. Our key analysis builds on an Extended Convex Lifting (ECL) framework that bridges nonconvex policy optimization and convex reformulations. The ECL constructions rely on non-strict Riccati inequalities that allow us to characterize global optimality. These insights reveal hidden convexity in mixed H2/H-infinity control and facilitate the design of scalable policy iteration methods in large-scale settings.

</details>


### [177] [A class of stochastic control problems with state constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04880)
*Tiziano De Angelis,Erik Ekström*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出线性二次最优控制问题的概率解法，处理具有状态约束的扩散过程控制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有状态约束的线性二次最优控制问题，需要在保持过程在特定区域内的同时最小化二次成本，传统方法难以处理此类约束问题

Method: 使用概率方法，在温和条件下找到值函数的概率表示和最优控制，最优控制采用强形式，适应于驱动布朗运动的滤波

Result: 获得了值函数的概率表示和最优控制策略，最优控制具有强形式，并在相关示例中给出了完全显式公式

Conclusion: 成功开发了处理状态约束线性二次最优控制问题的概率框架，在温和条件下提供了理论保证和显式解

Abstract: We obtain a probabilistic solution to linear-quadratic optimal control problems with state constraints. Given a closed set $\mathcal{D}\subseteq [0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^d$, a diffusion $X$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$ must be linearly controlled in order to keep the time-space process $(t,X_t)$ inside the set $\mathcal{C}:=([0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^d)\setminus\mathcal{D}$, while at the same time minimising an expected cost that depends on the state $(t,X_t)$ and is quadratic in the speed of the control exerted. We find a probabilistic representation for the value function and an optimal control under a set of mild sufficient conditions concerning the coefficients of the underlying dynamics and the regularity of the set $\mathcal{D}$. The optimally controlled dynamics is in strong form, in the sense that it is adapted to the filtration generated by the driving Brownian motion. Fully explicit formulae are presented in some relevant examples.

</details>


### [178] [Biquadratic SOS Rank and Double Zarankiewicz Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04912)
*Liqun Qi,Chunfeng Cui,Yi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文引入双Zarankiewicz数z₂(m,n)来刻画双二次形式的最大SOS秩，通过定义包含普通边和"双边"的二部图，建立了与广义C₄-自由图的联系，并计算了多个小参数下的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现双二次形式的最大SOS秩BSR(m,n)下界由经典Zarankiewicz数z(m,n)给出，但在4×3情况下存在间隙：BSR(4,3)≥8 > z(4,3)=7。这一间隙源于添加双线性形式平方的构造，需要新的理论框架来捕捉这种现象。

Method: 引入双Zarankiewicz数z₂(m,n)，定义包含普通边(1-边)和"双边"(2-边)的新型二部图，后者对应简单两项双线性形式的平方。证明若图G不包含广义C₄-环，则其对应的双简单双二次形式P_G的SOS秩等于|E₁|+|E₂|，从而建立BSR(m,n)≥z₂(m,n)的关系。

Result: 确定了多个小参数下的精确值：z₂(m,2)=m+1，z₂(2,n)=n+1，z₂(3,3)=6，z₂(4,3)=8，z₂(5,3)=9。对于4×4情况，证明10≤z₂(4,4)≤11，精确值作为开放问题。这些结果改进了BSR(4,4)和BSR(5,3)的已知下界。

Conclusion: 双Zarankiewicz数z₂(m,n)成功捕捉了经典Zarankiewicz数无法解释的间隙现象，建立了极值图论与SOS表示研究之间的新联系，为理解双二次形式的SOS秩提供了更精确的理论框架。

Abstract: The maximum sum-of-squares (SOS) rank of an $m\times n$ biquadratic form, denoted $\operatorname{BSR}(m,n)$, has recently been shown to be bounded below by the classical Zarankiewicz number $z(m,n)$, the maximum number of edges in a $C_4$-free bipartite graph. However, a gap was discovered for the $4\times3$ case: $\operatorname{BSR}(4,3)\ge 8 > z(4,3)=7$, arising from a construction that adds a square of a bilinear form $(x_4y_2+x_1y_3)^2$ to a $C_4$-free simple biquadratic form. This paper introduces the double Zarankiewicz number $z_2(m,n)$ to capture such phenomena. We define a new class of bipartite graphs that may contain both ordinary edges (1-edges) and ``double edges'' (2-edges), the latter corresponding to squares of simple two-term bilinear forms. A graph $G$ of this type yields a doubly simple biquadratic form $P_G$, and we prove that if $G$ contains no \emph{generalised $C_4$-cycle} then $\operatorname{sos}(P_G)=|E_1|+|E_2|$; consequently $\operatorname{BSR}(m,n)\ge z_2(m,n)$. We determine the exact value of $z_2(m,n)$ for several small parameters: $z_2(m,2)=m+1$, $z_2(2,n)=n+1$, $z_2(3,3)=6$, $z_2(4,3)=8$, and $z_2(5,3)=9$. For the $4\times4$ case we establish $10\le z_2(4,4)\le 11$, leaving the exact value as an open problem. These results improve the known lower bounds for $\operatorname{BSR}(4,4)$ and $\operatorname{BSR}(5,3)$. The paper concludes with a discussion of open questions and connections between extremal graph theory and the study of SOS representations.

</details>


### [179] [An Efficient Stochastic First-Order Algorithm for Nonconvex-Strongly Concave Minimax Optimization beyond Lipschitz Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04940)
*Yan Gao,Yongchao Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出NSGDA-M算法解决广义光滑条件下的非凸随机极小极大问题，在非凸-强凹情况下达到O(ε⁻⁴)梯度复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸随机极小极大算法大多基于标准Lipschitz光滑假设，本文研究广义光滑条件下的此类问题，旨在开发更高效的算法

Method: 提出NSGDA-M算法：内变量通过随机梯度上升更新，外变量通过带动量的归一化随机梯度下降更新

Result: 当目标函数为非凸-强凹时，NSGDA-M以期望O(ε⁻⁴)和高概率O(ε⁻⁴(log(1/δ))³/²)的随机梯度评估找到ε-稳定点

Conclusion: NSGDA-M在广义光滑条件下有效解决了非凸随机极小极大问题，理论分析和数值实验验证了其优越性能

Abstract: In recent years, nonconvex minimax problems have attracted significant attention due to their broad applications in machine learning, including generative adversarial networks, robust optimization and adversarial training. Most existing algorithms for nonconvex stochastic minimax problems are developed under the standard Lipschitz smoothness assumption. In this paper, we study stochastic minimax problems under a generalized smoothness condition and propose an algorithm, NSGDA-M, which simultaneously updates the inner variable by stochastic gradient ascent and updates the outer variable by normalized stochastic gradient descent with momentum. When the objective function is nonconvex-strongly concave, we show that NSGDA-M finds an $ε$-stationary point of the primal function within $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ stochastic gradient evaluations in expectation, and $\mathcal{O}\left(ε^{-4}(\log(\frac{1}δ))^{3/2}\right)$ stochastic gradient evaluations in high probability, where $δ\in (0,1)$ is the failure probability. We verify the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm through numerical experiments on a distributionally robust optimization problem.

</details>


### [180] [Stochastic Optimal Feedforward-Feedback Control for Partially Observable Sensorimotor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04994)
*Bastien Berret,Frédéric Jean*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个连续时间框架，将前馈和反馈控制结合，通过统计线性化将随机问题转化为确定性优化，应用于人类神经力学，证明肌肉共收缩是适应任务需求的最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工程和生物系统的鲁棒控制依赖于前馈和反馈机制的整合。神经运动控制中，前馈肌肉共收缩与感觉驱动的反馈校正共同确保稳定行为。然而，为部分可观测、随机、非线性、高维系统推导通用的连续时间框架来确定最优控制策略仍是一个巨大的计算挑战。

Method: 引入一个扩展邻近最优控制的框架，使前馈计划能明确考虑反馈不确定性和延迟。使用统计线性化将随机问题转化为近似等价的确定性优化，在可处理的增强状态空间中保留关键非线性，提供机制可解释性和近似保真度的理论保证。

Result: 将该框架应用于人类神经力学，证明肌肉共收缩是适应任务需求的最优适应策略，考虑到感觉运动系统的特性。

Conclusion: 结果为神经运动控制提供了计算基础，并为非线性随机系统的控制提供了一个可推广的工具。

Abstract: Robust control of complex engineered and biological systems hinges on the integration of feedforward and feedback mechanisms. This is exemplified in neural motor control, where feedforward muscle co-contraction complements sensory-driven feedback corrections to ensure stable behaviors. However, deriving a general continuous-time framework to determine such optimal control policies for partially observable, stochastic, nonlinear, and high-dimensional systems remains a formidable computational challenge. Here, we introduce a framework that extends neighboring optimal control by enabling the feedforward plan to explicitly account for feedback uncertainties and latencies. Using statistical linearization, we transform the stochastic problem into an approximately equivalent deterministic optimization within a tractable, augmented state space that retains critical nonlinearities, offering both mechanistic interpretability and theoretical guarantees on approximation fidelity. We apply this framework to human neuromechanics, demonstrating that muscle co-contraction emerges as an optimal adaptation to task demands, given the characteristics of our sensorimotor system. Our results provide a computational foundation for neuromotor control and a generalizable tool for the control of nonlinear stochastic systems.

</details>


### [181] [A Second-Order Algorithm Based on Affine Scaling Interior-Point Methods for nonlinear minimization with bound constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05022)
*Yonggang Pei,Yubing Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将HSODM扩展到带边界约束的非线性优化问题，提出基于仿射缩放内点法的二阶算法SOBASIP，具有O(ε^(-3/2))全局复杂度和局部超线性收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSODM在无约束优化中表现出色，但需要扩展到带边界约束的问题。边界约束在实际应用中很常见，需要开发高效算法

Method: 提出SOBASIP算法：1)引入仿射矩阵构造仿射缩放子问题；2)使用齐次化技术将子问题转化为普通齐次模型(OHM)，即特征值问题；3)从OHM最优解构造下降方向；4)使用回溯线搜索确定新迭代点

Result: 理论分析表明SOBASIP达到O(ε^(-3/2))全局迭代复杂度寻找ε-近似二阶稳定点，在特定条件下局部超线性收敛。数值结果验证了方法的良好性能

Conclusion: 成功将HSODM扩展到边界约束问题，提出的SOBASIP算法具有理论保证和实际有效性，为带边界约束的非线性优化提供了高效二阶方法

Abstract: The homogeneous second-order descent method (HSODM: Zhang et al. in Mathematics of Operations Research (2025)) was initially proposed for unconstrained optimization problems. HSODM shows excellent performance with respect to the global complexity rate among a certain broad class of second-order methods. In this paper, we extend HSODM to solve nonlinear optimization problems with bound constraints and propose a second-order algorithm based on affine scaling interior-point methods (SOBASIP). In each iteration, an appropriate affine matrix is introduced to construct an affine scaling subproblem based on the optimality conditions of the problem. To obtain a valid descent direction similar to HSODM, we utilize the homogenization technique to transform the scaling subproblem into an Ordinary Homogeneous Model (OHM), which is essentially an eigenvalue problem that can be solved efficiently. The descent direction is constructed from the optimal solution to the OHM, and then backtracking line search is used to determine the new iteration point. Theoretical analysis establishes that SOBASIP achieves a global iteration complexity of O(epsilon^(-3/2)) for finding an epsilon-approximate second-order stationary point and converges locally at a superlinear rate under certain conditions. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method exhibits satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [182] [Robust adaptive NMPC using ellipsoidal tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05029)
*Johannes Buerger,Mark Cannon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种计算高效的基于学习的非线性模型预测控制算法，采用椭球管方法处理模型不确定性和扰动，保证约束满足和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对学习型控制系统中存在模型参数未知和加性有界扰动的问题，需要开发既能保证安全性又具有计算效率的非线性模型预测控制算法。

Method: 采用基函数仿射组合表示系统模型，通过连续线性化处理非线性，使用椭球集合量化线性化误差、模型误差和扰动带来的状态不确定性，结合回溯线搜索确保鲁棒性。

Result: 数值模拟显示椭球嵌入模型不确定性的方法在系统维度增加时具有良好的可扩展性，算法能够保证递归可行性和输入到状态实际稳定性。

Conclusion: 提出的椭球管基NMPC算法为学习型控制系统提供了一种计算高效且具有安全保证的控制框架，能够有效处理模型不确定性和扰动。

Abstract: We propose a computationally efficient nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) algorithm for safe, learning-based control. The system model is represented as an affine combination of basis functions with unknown parameters, and is subject to additive set-bounded disturbances. Our algorithm employs successive linearization around nominal predicted trajectories and accounts for uncertainties in predicted states due to linearization, model errors, and disturbances using ellipsoidal sets. The ellipsoidal tube-based approach ensures that constraints on control inputs and system states are satisfied. Robustness to uncertainty is ensured using bounds on linearization errors and a backtracking line search. We show that the ellipsoidal embedding of model uncertainty scales favourably with system dimensions in numerical simulations. The algorithm incorporates set membership parameter estimation, and provides guarantees of recursive feasibility and input-to-state practical stability.

</details>


### [183] [Solution of a bilevel optimistic scheduling problem on parallel machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05107)
*Quentin Schau,Olivier Ploton,Vincent T'kindt,Han Hoogeveen,Federico Della Croce,Jippe Hoogeveen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了乐观双层优化框架下的均匀并行机调度问题，其中领导者最小化加权延迟作业数，跟随者最小化总完工时间，证明了问题的强NP难性并提出了动态规划、MIP建模和分支定界算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双层优化框架下的调度问题具有重要的实际应用价值，特别是在工业4.0背景下。传统的单层调度模型无法完全描述现实生产系统中存在的层次决策结构，其中上层管理者（领导者）和下层执行者（跟随者）有不同的优化目标。这种领导者-跟随者交互模式在智能制造、供应链管理等场景中普遍存在。

Method: 1. 理论分析：通过从数值三维匹配问题归约，证明了该问题是强NP难的；2. 算法设计：提出了中等指数时间的动态规划算法；3. 混合整数规划：建立了简洁的MIP模型；4. 分支定界算法：嵌入列生成方法计算下界。

Result: 1. 理论结果：证明了问题的强NP难性；2. 算法性能：提出的方法能够处理最多80个作业和4台机器的实例；3. 局限性：对于更大规模的问题，现有方法难以有效求解。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将双层优化框架应用于均匀并行机调度问题，建立了理论复杂性分析并开发了多种求解方法。虽然算法在处理中等规模实例时表现良好，但大规模问题的求解仍然具有挑战性，这为未来的研究方向提供了启示。

Abstract: We consider the uniform parallel machines scheduling problem in the context of optimistic bilevel optimization, where two speed options are considered. In this scenario, the leader aims to minimize the weighted number of tardy jobs, while the follower seeks to minimize the total completion time on a set of uniform machines. This problem has practical applications in Industry 4.0. We show that this problem is NP-hard in the strong sense by providing a reduction from the Numerical 3-Dimensional Matching problem and we provide a moderately exponential-time dynamic programming algorithm. The problem is solved by means of a concise MIP formulation and a branch-and-bound algorithm that embeds a column generation approach for the lower bound computation. Computational experiments are presented for instances with up to 80 jobs and 4 machines while larger problems are out of reach for the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [184] [Optimization with Parametric Variational Inequality Constraints on a Moving Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05196)
*Xiaojun Chen,Jin Zhang,Yixuan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出针对移动集合上参数变分不等式约束优化问题的理论分析和求解算法，证明解的存在性、有界性和度量正则性，并开发基于平滑近似的隐式梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学规划与均衡约束（MPEC）研究大多假设均衡约束仅函数含参数，而本文关注参数同时出现在函数和相关集合中的参数变分不等式（PVI）约束优化问题，这类问题在投资组合管理等实际应用中更为普遍。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明PVI解函数关于上层决策变量的Lipschitz连续性、优化问题解集非空有界性、约束度量正则性自动成立；2) 算法设计：提出基于PVI平滑近似的平滑隐式梯度算法（SIGA），证明其收敛到优化问题的稳定点。

Result: 1) 理论结果：建立了PVI约束优化问题的基本性质，无需额外假设即可刻画稳定点；2) 算法性能：SIGA收敛性得到证明，在真实数据的投资组合管理问题上验证了算法效率。

Conclusion: 本文为参数同时出现在函数和集合中的PVI约束优化问题提供了完整的理论框架和有效算法，填补了现有MPEC研究的空白，并在实际应用中展示了良好性能。

Abstract: This paper focuses on optimization problems constrained by Parametric Variational Inequalities (PVI) defined on a moving set. Unlike most existing works on mathematical programs with equilibrium constraints, the equilibrium constraints have parameters not only in the function but also in the related set. We show that the solution function of the PVI is Lipschitz continuous with respect to the upper-level decision variables and the solution set of the optimization problem is nonempty and bounded. Moreover, we prove that the metric regularity of the constraints holds automatically, which allow us to characterize stationary points without any additional assumptions. A Smoothing Implicit Gradient Algorithm (SIGA) is proposed based on the smoothing approximation of the PVI. We prove the convergence of SIGA to a stationary point of the optimization problem and numerically validate the efficiency of SIGA by portfolio management problems with real data.

</details>


### [185] [Integral Formulation and the Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles-Type Principle for Prox-Regular Sweeping Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05376)
*Juan Guillermo Garrido,Emilio Vilches*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了希尔伯特空间中由时变一致近端正则集驱动的扫掠过程，允许移动约束具有有界变差间断性。提出了新的积分公式，证明了其与标准微分测度公式的等价性，并建立了Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles型变分特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究近端正则（通常非凸）框架下的扫掠过程，处理移动约束具有有界变差间断性的情况。标准微分测度公式在非凸情况下存在局限性，需要开发新的积分公式来统一有界变差解的概念。

Method: 引入新的积分公式：基于全局变分不等式，测试对象为连续容许轨迹，包含反映近端法锥次单调性的二次修正项。在移动集满足一定正则性条件下（包括时间下半连续性、一致近端正则性、选择-延拓性质），证明该积分公式与微分测度公式等价。

Result: 1. 证明了新积分公式与微分测度公式在近端正则框架下的等价性，统一了有界变差解的概念。
2. 建立了Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles型变分特征：通过近端正则变分残差，解是使残差达到最大值（零）的轨迹。
3. 证明了稳定性结果：具有消失残差的容许轨迹的一致极限是极限扫掠过程的解。

Conclusion: 提出的变分框架为近端正则非凸设置下的稳定性和近似分析提供了稳健工具。新积分公式统一了有界变差解的概念，变分特征为解的存在性和稳定性分析提供了新方法。

Abstract: We study sweeping processes in a Hilbert space driven by time-dependent uniformly prox-regular sets, allowing the moving constraint to exhibit discontinuities of bounded variation. We introduce a new integral formulation for bounded-variation trajectories, given by a global variational inequality tested against continuous admissible trajectories, and we compare it with the standard differential-measure formulation, in which the differential measure of the trajectory is constrained by the proximal normal cone. In the prox-regular (generally nonconvex) framework, the variational inequality necessarily includes a quadratic correction term reflecting the hypomonotonicity of proximal normal cones.
  Under mild regularity assumptions on the moving set, including lower semicontinuity in time, uniform prox-regularity of the values, and a selection-extension property guaranteeing a rich class of test trajectories (satisfied, for instance, in the convex case and for bounded prox-regular sets), we prove that the new integral formulation is equivalent to the differential-measure formulation. This yields a unified bounded-variation notion of solution for prox-regular sweeping processes.
  We further establish a Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles-type variational characterization via a prox-regular variational residual: the residual is nonpositive along every admissible trajectory, and solutions are exactly those trajectories for which this residual attains its maximal value, namely zero. As a consequence, we prove a stability result: a uniform limit of admissible trajectories with vanishing residual is a solution of the limit sweeping process. The resulting variational framework provides a robust tool for stability and approximation analyses in the prox-regular, nonconvex setting.

</details>


### [186] [The Inverse Micromechanics Problem given Dielectric Constants for Isotropic Composites with Spherical Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05460)
*Athindra Pavan,Swaroop Darbha,Bjorn Birgisson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将凸优化引入Eshelby基逆向微力学问题，聚焦于使用Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka模型根据复合材料及其各组分的介电常数确定微观结构信息，特别是球形夹杂物的体积分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是开发一种基于凸优化的系统方法来处理Eshelby基逆向微力学问题。传统正向微力学问题根据组分性质和微观结构信息确定复合材料有效性质，而逆向问题则需要在已知复合材料及其所有组分性质的情况下确定微观结构信息。作者选择Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka模型是因为其相对简单且对球状夹杂物有闭合形式解析解。

Method: 方法包括：1) 使用Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka模型处理各向同性复合材料，假设夹杂物分布保持各向同性；2) 将逆向微力学问题（确定组分体积分数）表述为线性规划问题；3) 通过凸优化视角分析逆向问题及其重要变体；4) 研究色散材料、测量噪声与获得体积分割质量之间的关系。

Result: 展示了凸优化在解决Eshelby基逆向微力学问题中的有效性，特别是在确定复合材料组分体积分数方面。论文还展示了关于色散材料、测量噪声与获得体积分割质量之间关系的有希望结果，证明了该方法在存在实验不确定性和材料复杂性情况下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 凸优化是研究Eshelby基逆向微力学问题的有前景工具。该方法能够有效解决确定微观结构信息的逆向问题，特别是对于球形夹杂物的各向同性复合材料。论文还讨论了凸优化在逆向微力学中的适用范围和潜力，为处理更复杂的微观结构表征问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this article, convex optimization is introduced as a promising tool to study Eshelby based inverse micromechanics problems. The focus is on inverse micromechanics using the Eshelby-Mori-Tanaka model given the dielectric constants of the composite material and of all of its components. The model is exactly the same for the conductivity properties as well. This choice of model is made since the model is fairly simple and has a closed form analytical solution for the case of spheroidal inclusions as well. The forward or direct micromechanics problem deals with the determination of effective properties of a composite material given the properties of its components and microstructural information. The focus is on isotropic composites and the distribution of inclusions is assumed to be such that this holds. The inverse micromechanics problem considered in this paper deals with the determination of microstructural information given the properties of the composite material and all of its components. Since in this paper, isotropy of the composite and only spherical inclusions are considered, the goal is to determine just volume fractions of the components of the composite material. The inverse problem is formulated as a Linear Programming problem and is solved. Before this, the inverse problem and certain important variants of it are examined through the lens of convex optimization. Lastly, promising results regarding the relationship between dispersive materials, noise in measurements, and quality of obtained volumetric splits are showcased. The scope of the use of convex optimization in inverse micromechanics is discussed.

</details>
